## Audit: K2_v1 task-snapshot run

**Outcome:** The student completed normally and committed an implementation, but the official result was **formally unsolved**: F2P 52/53, P2P 8/8, binary reward 0. The failed F2P test expected nonempty output from an empty snapshot list; the command returned an empty string. The verifier did not time out. This is distinct from the student's local console-test failure and from its successful local snapshot probe.

### Exploration and changes

The `main_agent` created branch `feature/task-snapshots`, listed the project, searched for monitor, command, and completion interfaces, and read the monitor, types, terminal and web files (events 1–12). Before editing, it checked for `Monitor.capture_snapshot`; the assertion failed because the method was absent (events 13, 17). Several reads and search results were truncated, so their unseen content cannot establish what was inspected.

The executable changes introduced snapshot records and capture, storage, eviction, lookup, stack, and diff methods in `aiomonitor/monitor.py`; terminal commands in `aiomonitor/termui/commands.py`; and routes and a page in `aiomonitor/webui/app.py` and `aiomonitor/webui/templates/snapshots.html` (events 21–28; final patch). After inspecting task identity, the student also added an object ID to termination information in `aiomonitor/task.py` and `aiomonitor/types.py`, and used identities in snapshot diffs (events 33–41). The patch routes live capture through the monitored loop when necessary and returns deep copies from snapshot lookup. Commit `e718709` contained six files and 320 insertions (events 47–48).

The identity addition and the web and terminal surfaces are real additional implementation work, not merely claims. The supplied record does not establish that any of them was unnecessary: the official tests cover identity, CLI and web behavior. Nor does it establish that every design choice was required. One observable omission is that `snapshot list` emits nothing when there are no entries, as both the final patch and official failure show.

### Validation, regressions and cost

The first combined lint/test output is truncated (events 29–30). After formatting, imports succeeded but the full local pytest run failed `test_monitor_with_console`; the student retried that test and it failed again (events 31–32, 44–46). The final message claims missing console output and a transport-endpoint error, and seven other local tests passed (event 49); the displayed failure details are truncated, and no pre-change run establishes whether this was a regression or its cause. A later filtered local monitor run passed five tests, excluding console (events 40–42). Lint passed (events 33–34, 39–41).

A separate executable probe captured a task and stack, checked removal and unnamed eviction, and exercised selected web endpoints, including a missing-ID 404; it printed success (events 43, 45). It did not exercise the empty CLI list or demonstrate all failure classes. Official evaluation subsequently identified that exact missing case: `tests/test_snapshot.py::test_cli_snapshot_list_empty` asserted that output must not be blank. The official result reports 52/53 F2P and 8/8 P2P; local checks are not a substitute for those results or for full success.

The run ended with `end_turn`, not a budget or technical stop. Reported costs were 24 requested tool calls with returns, 13 model requests, 866,556 input and 6,013 output tokens, 763.203 seconds wall time and 487.714 seconds SDK time. The SDK `total_cost_usd` of 4.483105 is a proxy estimate, **not an actual CLI fee**; no actual dollar fee is established. Formatting, repeated console testing, extra identity work and the broad implementation all consumed resources, but the record does not quantify their separate costs or show that the repeated failure was caused by the patch.

### Capability assessment

Four capabilities were loaded in `loaded_capabilities.md`. Their overlap with behavior is observational, not proof of adoption or causation; an ordinary student could independently perform the same work. There is no B0 trajectory in the supplied record, so no baseline behavior or comparative adoption claim can be made. In particular, B0 would have no library to attribute knowledge to.

1. **Inspect state across transitions:** The capture/diff implementation and probe directly preserve and compare task state before and after cancellation (events 21, 39, 43–45). The probe checks a removed task, but does not inspect every downstream consumer's actual input or all cross-loop and continuation cases. The student claims snapshot checks passed (event 49), which is narrower than proof of complete transition coverage.
2. **Check identity and failure feedback end to end:** The identity mapping is executable, and the web probe checks an unknown snapshot response (events 39–41, 43–45). Official identity tests passed, but the visible probe does not trace a live-to-terminated identity round trip or systematically distinguish worker failures. The terminal `where` command combines invalid snapshot and task feedback; no general end-to-end failure taxonomy is demonstrated.
3. **Carry input contracts into later use:** The patch accepts IDs from CLI and web requests and converts snapshot IDs for lookup, while the probe sends JSON IDs to later consumers (events 21–25, 43–45). This is limited consistency across an API boundary, not evidence of a learned schema, input ordering, alias coverage, or systematic missing/extra-input validation. The empty-list failure is contrary to complete consumer-facing contract coverage.
4. **Preserve meaning when changing representation:** The patch transforms formatted task information into stored records and JSON; the probe retrieves a stack and endpoints return successful statuses (events 21–24, 43–45). These actions are consistent with preserving some information, but no semantic-equivalence or idempotence check is visible. The representation capability's formatter/tokenizer-specific method is not directly demonstrated.

Truncated edit commands and diff output limit conclusions about what was visible at those moments; the supplied final patch permits assessment of the resulting code, not of unseen reasoning. The student's final assertions are claims, corroborated only to the extent of the cited tool returns and official results.

The committed patch added `DataVar` and change records in `/app/statemachine/data.py`, declaration validation in `state.py`, per-machine storage and accessors in `statemachine.py`, and lifecycle, callback, history and macrostep handling in the engines (31–54, 55–78, 79–82). Later edits added literal SCXML state data and diagram annotations (116–127, 132–143). These are actual changes, not proof that every specified semantic works. For example, the SCXML parser silently skips expressions that are not Python literals. The task expressly requested SCXML and diagram support, so those extensions cannot be classified as unnecessary merely because they widened the diff. No existing test file was changed; the committed artifact had 15 files changed (164–165).

**Validation and regressions.** The first post-change reproduction hit a separate invalid trap-state fixture (80, 83); the corrected invocation printed no failure, but the chained full local suite timed out (85, 90). Focused checks reported 8 passes (96, 98), then 102 passes (108, 110–112), and later 78 passes and 142 skips (145, 149, 153–155). Ruff and the later mypy check passed (153–155). A temporary script exercised a synchronous entry, typed mutation, pickle, exit and reset (158, 160–162); it was removed before commit (163–164). A second full local suite also timed out (157, 159), so neither timeout is a normal full-suite pass. The student accurately qualified that gap in event 165, but its claim of implementation is not a full-success result.

Official F2P exposed three failures: both sync and async transitions to a state without data, and backward compatibility for an initially active state without data, returned `{}` where `None` was expected. Official P2P had one failure; the supplied verifier tail shows a 60-second pytest timeout in event matching, but does not establish its cause or even identify the test in the visible excerpt. It should not be attributed to a particular patch change. The tail and several read returns are truncated; unseen details remain unknown.

**Capabilities and attribution.** The supplied `loaded_capabilities.md` contains four capabilities and metadata says it was in the actual prompt and first bridge request. ## Audit finding

The HT_Exploration run produced a substantial but **formally unsolved** implementation. Official evaluation reported F2P **69/72**, P2P **1285/1286**, and binary reward **0**. The visible verifier tail identifies three F2P failures: active states without declared data returned `{}` rather than `None`, in sync, async, and backward-compatibility cases (`tests/test_state_data.py:428,556`). It also shows a 60-second pytest timeout in a transition callback path, but the tail does not establish its cause or identify it sufficiently to explain the single P2P failure. The verifier itself completed in 217.72 seconds, below its 3,600-second timeout. Source: supplied `formal_evaluation` and `eval_result.json` at the run path below.

## Exploration and implementation

`main_agent` created `feat/state-owned-data`, searched lifecycle, history, factory and SCXML locations, and read the state, machine, engine, event and SCXML modules (events 1–26). The first executable probe established that `State(data=...)` raised `TypeError` (3, 8); a more specific temporary reproduction initially failed because `DataVar` was not exported (27–30).

The agent added `statemachine/data.py`, state declarations and exports, and per-machine storage, accessors, validation, change records and merged scope (`main_agent` 31–46, 47–59; final patch in `/app/statemachine/{data.py,state.py,statemachine.py}`). It modified sync/base and async lifecycle paths for entry, exit, history snapshots and macrostep clearing, and injected callback data (`main_agent` 48–70; `/app/statemachine/engines/` and `event_data.py`). It also added literal SCXML data parsing and diagram annotations (103–116; `/app/statemachine/io/scxml/` and `contrib/diagram/extract.py`). These integration changes were requested by the public task; the visible record does not establish that they were unnecessary. A no-op Mermaid edit did consume a failed tool call (109, 117).

The final patch implements `_data_scope` as a newly merged dictionary. A local callback probe showed that assigning `state_data['x'] = 4` left stored data at `{'x': 1}` (161–164). This is a directly observed limitation, not merely the student’s final claim. The student disclosed it and the incomplete verification in event 177. The agent committed 13 files as `3efc43f` (175–176); the temporary reproduction was removed rather than retained as a regression test.

The student searched field metadata, aliases, strict-key handling and code generation, then read `/app/mashumaro/helper.py`, `/app/mashumaro/core/meta/code/builder.py`, mixin and nested pack/unpack code (main_agent events 3–12, 19–36). The committed patch changed `mashumaro/helper.py` and `mashumaro/core/meta/code/builder.py`: it added flatten, prefix and rename metadata; recursively resolved child wire keys and collisions; and generated packing and unpacking handling, including optional values and strict extra-key allowances. The helper also unconditionally returned the three new default metadata entries—the change identified by the official failing test. The commit reported 103 insertions and two deletions (event 74); no persistent tests or documentation were added in the visible change set.

Local iteration exposed and repaired specific problems: optional empty input initially raised `InvalidFieldValue` (events 39–42); renamed aliases initially triggered a duplicate-key error, then strict child input reconstructed an unwanted extra key (events 47–54); and default child-alias round-trip initially failed before another mapping change (events 59–62). Local checks subsequently covered prefix/strict behavior, collisions and parent aliases (events 64–70). These are observations of those examples, not proof of all combinations.

The student ran existing tests and inspected the diff, but local coverage was constrained: `ciso8601`, `yaml`, and the `mocker` fixture were missing (events 42, 45, 57, 65, 72). The final local alias and generics run passed 27 tests (events 73–74). The student’s final summary at event 75 accurately claims those local checks and limitations, but does not claim the later official outcome. It did not run the existing `tests/test_helper.py` locally, where the official verifier found the regression.

The loaded library describes optional guidance, not evidence of prior implementations. **Capability 4, translating contracts into observable checks**, has the clearest behavioral match: executable positive and negative examples, existing regression checks, diff review and branch/commit verification are visible. There is no visible requirement-to-check matrix, comprehensive entry-point coverage, or persistent test addition. The final message is a student claim supported only to the extent of the cited returns; finite local checks did not establish full success.

**Capability 2, persisted schemas as an inference contract**, is at most an analogy: the student examined aliases and mapped names in both serialization directions, with strict-key checks. No persisted training/inference schema, fresh-process artifact reload, or source-consistency policy is shown; the capability itself cautions against importing such policies without evidence. **Capability 1, stateful control flow and failure propagation**, has a limited overlap in tracing nested pack/unpack calls and observing exceptions, but no workflow lifecycle map, identifier correlation, stream/resumption checks or cycle test is visible. **Capability 3, syntax structure and source fidelity**, is not substantively applicable to this dataclass serialization change; reading and generating code does not demonstrate its parser/formatter guidance. Some read results and the initial implementation command are truncated (events 11–12, 37, 58), so their omitted contents cannot establish additional behavior. None of these matches proves library causation: ordinary task-driven development could produce the same actions.

The patch's unconditional default metadata additions were unnecessary for preserving the existing helper return contract and caused the observed P2P regression. Repeated locally blocked test attempts consumed effort without completing those suites; they cannot be treated as test failures in the implementation or as proof that all unrun cases failed. No repair after official evaluation is visible.

* **Preserve usable state during continuation.** Directly consistent behavior: the student probed whether flattened deserialization retained independent parent and child values, including multiple children (117–118, 123–124), and repaired default-factory handling after a failure (133–138). The default metadata regression is contrary to preserving an existing consumer contract. These probes do not establish all nested or partial-state cases.

This addresses key identity across serialization, not object lifecycle identity. There is no claim that the library caused it and no visible lifecycle or eviction test; those aspects are not applicable or unassessable here.
* **Reconcile representations without weakening contracts.** Directly consistent behavior: the student inspected existing adapters, reproduced a mismatch, and tested aliases, optional values, nested flattening and extra-key-related behavior (17–22, 74–80, 91–94, 105–114, 133–138). The observed exploration, edits and tests are baseline student behavior, not evidence of knowledge adoption or library causation. There are no relevant loaded-capability claims to assess. That is full success under the reported verifier, not normal student completion: the student exhausted its budget, its final metadata says `incomplete`/`CancelledError`, and no commit or final student response is visible. The first formal evaluation subsequently tested the identical saved deadline patch without another student or model call.

## Exploration and changes

`main_agent` created the requested branch and located the facade and name-layout implementation (events 1–2). It inspected mapping, crown construction, loader generation, overlays and schema handling (events 3–38). A minimal executable reproduction showed that `name_mapping(aliases=...)` initially raised `TypeError` (events 11, 13).

The final patch changes five production files under `/app/src/adaptix/_internal/morphing/`:

- `facade/provider.py` adds and normalizes `aliases` and `alias_style`, and documents their load-only purpose; `facade/retort.py` supplies defaults (events 39–41, 106–108).
- `name_layout/component.py` adds overlay fields and merging, constructs literal and style-generated aliases for input paths, and checks collisions; `model/crown_definitions.py` stores aliases on input field crowns (events 42–52, 63–65, 71–73).
- `model/loader_gen.py` recognizes alias keys, generates fallback/conflict and trail handling, adjusts missing-field behavior, and exposes typed alias properties and required alternatives in input schema (events 53–61, 105–107, 111–114). The final saved patch is the source for the complete code where event 55 is truncated.

No test file is included in the final patch. The student used inline checks and existing tests instead. It did not visibly commit, contrary to the public task's explicit instruction; event 117 still shows modified working-tree files.

## Validation, regressions and costs

The first implementation caused an **import regression**: an unhashable dictionary in the overlay prevented `adaptix` from importing (events 57, 62). After a hash-related edit, the original alias reproduction passed (events 63–66). The first full local pytest run exited 1, but its failure details are truncated (events 67, 69); its cause cannot be inferred. After an overlay-default edit, 604 focused existing tests passed (events 71–74). A later full local suite returned success, although its summary is truncated (events 109–110), and 604 focused tests passed again after further edits (events 115, 117).

Inline checks showed primary, literal and generated keys loading; conflict errors across debug modes; primary-name dumping; and an error trail using the actual alias (events 75–78). A schema check initially used a nonexistent public import (events 79–80). Two later schema commands exceeded 120 seconds and were stopped (events 85–88, 95–98); a 20-second diagnostic sampled execution during import, not a demonstrated schema-generation cause (events 99–100). A subsequent command did produce schema with primary and alias properties, but still required the primary key (events 103–104). The student then changed required-alternative schema generation (events 112, 114). Its final combined alias/schema check was requested at event 116, **with no return in the supplied record**; its outcome is unassessable.

The repeated schema attempts and resource inspection (events 81–104) consumed time beyond the successful local checks; the record does not establish that every step was necessary. The import break was a demonstrated temporary regression, while the truncated first suite failure and timeouts have unknown causes. The official verifier later applied the 12,646-byte patch and reported base and new pytest return codes 0, including 2812 base passes and 28 skips, plus 44 new passes; its separately reported P2P count is 2738/2738. These results support the formal grade, not universal correctness.

Reported costs are 59 tool calls requested, 58 returned, 28 model requests, 2,020,669 input tokens and 7,802 output tokens, with 2,843.717 seconds end-to-end and 470.719 seconds verifier elapsed. The student budget was 1,800 seconds. No actual CLI dollar fee is provided; an SDK cost estimate, if available, would only be a proxy, not billing.

## Capability-specific assessment

1. **Cross-interface contracts.** Directly consistent behavior includes inspection of facade, input layout, generated loading and schema boundaries, plus conflict and trail probes (events 4–8, 11–32, 83–84). The student claims load-only aliases, conflict detection and input schema properties in event 95; the patch supports those implementation claims, while local schema and dump-equivalence execution are not visible. No explicit contract matrix or comparison of equivalent operations across interfaces is shown. Truncated reads limit assessment of the exploration, not the visible patch.

2. **Lifecycle, recovery and recursive execution.** The overlay default/hash regression and repair show iterative correction (events 56–64, 71–76), but this capability principally concerns stateful record lifecycle, partial recovery and recursive delegation. Those behaviors are not evidenced here; ordinary debugging is not evidence of that specialized workflow. No student claim of such a workflow appears. Truncated outputs do not justify inventing one.

3. **Structured-data boundaries.** The generated loader distinguishes missing required and defaulted fields and appends the resolved key to error trails; probes show missing and invalid inputs and conflict errors (events 42, 83–84; final patch). The student’s general implementation claim is not a claim that all nested, collection or extra-key cases were locally exercised. No local nested-partial or collection-failure probe is visible, and source-formatting guidance is not applicable. Truncated code and test returns prevent a complete local-coverage assessment.

4. **Training schema across serving boundaries.** This model-serving capability is not directly applicable to Adaptix. Its narrower alias/conflict-check theme overlaps with the executable alias probes (events 83–86), but there is no evidence of persisted training schemas, prediction/export paths or HTTP validation, nor a student claim to have performed them. The overlap cannot establish adoption of the serving workflow.

## Exploration and changes

## Capability assessment and validation

**Trace across boundaries.** Direct behavior consistent with this capability includes examining the facade-to-layout-to-loader path and implementing changes across those layers, then testing public loads and an error trail (events 11–25, 27–28, 43–44, 49–50). The patch also changes schema generation, but the visible record shows schema-interface searches, not an executed schema assertion or a demonstrated full load/dump round trip (events 36, 43, 46). A complete cross-boundary verification is therefore not visible. Several read outputs were truncated (events 8, 17–20, 24–25), limiting what can be inferred about what the student inspected.

**Reproduce and inspect behavior.** The same simple reproduction changed from an unexpected-keyword error to `Model(value=1)` after edits and a hashability fix (events 7, 10, 28, 30, 33–34). Executable probes exercised literal and style aliases, duplicate keys, bad values, missing fields and debug modes (events 43–44); a later probe observed the actual alias in the error trail (events 49–50). These are concrete checks, not merely the student’s final claim. They do not by themselves cover every requested interaction.

**Verify transformations beyond an example.** This capability is only partially analogous: alias loading intentionally changes accepted input, so formatter-style semantic preservation and repeated-application idempotence are not necessarily the contract. The student did use varied inputs and regression suites, consistent with its broader advice (events 43–47). No repeated-transformation or idempotence test is visible; absence is not itself a failure against this task.

**Report verification precisely.** The student’s final statement (event 59) is a claim, supported by local results: 199 focused tests passed after the final adjustment (events 53, 55), while 1322 morphing tests passed before that adjustment (events 45, 47). The student correctly qualified the timing. The official F2P/P2P results came separately from the verifier, not from that statement. Early failure output was truncated (events 37, 40), so the complete underlying diagnostics are not visible; the observed follow-up repairs should not be treated as proof of every failure’s cause.

## Regressions, extra work and costs

## Exploration and implementation

The event 13–14 public-interface probe established only that `aliases` was absent: `name_mapping` rejected the keyword. It did not establish an existing behavioral defect.

The final patch changes five files under `src/adaptix/_internal/morphing/`: `facade/provider.py` adds and normalizes `aliases` and `alias_style`; `facade/retort.py` supplies defaults; `model/crown_definitions.py` retains aliases on input field crowns; `name_layout/component.py` merges overlays, generates aliases and checks collisions; and `model/loader_gen.py` resolves input keys, handles conflicts and trails, recognizes aliases for extra-key processing, and exposes them in input JSON Schema. The final patch, rather than the truncated commands alone, establishes these changes. The student committed them as `15dfd9cf` on `feature/name-mapping-aliases` (events 61–62).

The student searched twice for schema examples without visible matches (events 54–58); no direct schema-specific assertion or durable new test file appears in the supplied patch. Schema work and those searches were additional work beyond the basic alias reproduction, though schema exposure was requested; the record does not show that the searches were necessary.

The SDK's $7.4157 `total_cost_usd` is a proxy estimate, **not an actual CLI fee**. The separately reported SDK session duration is 1,122.471 seconds; these timing measures have different scopes.

Subsequent probes reported the expected missing/conflicting keys for the exercised cases (55–56).

That is compatible with distinguishing identity from a surface label, but no lifecycle/snapshot transition or independent-state identity test is shown. ## Exploration, changes and validation

These areas relate to stated requirements, although the record does not show a concrete finding or implementation from those reads. ## Audit conclusion

The K1_v1 student made and committed a substantial but formally incomplete implementation. The official verifier reported **69/72 F2P** and **1285/1286 P2P**, with binary reward **0**. The student ended normally with `end_turn` and explicitly acknowledged incomplete verification; the full-suite and type-check **local commands**, not the overall student run, timed out. The official verifier itself did not time out.

The four loaded capabilities are available in `loaded_capabilities.md` at the supplied run path. The behaviors below are consistent with portions of that guidance, not evidence that the library caused them: an ordinary base student could have performed the same work. No B0 trajectory or baseline metrics were supplied, so no baseline comparison or knowledge adoption is assessable.

## Exploration and changes

`main_agent` created `feat/state-owned-data` from `main`, searched lifecycle and data-model code, read `/app/AGENTS.md`, and inspected state, engine, callback, SCXML and diagram interfaces (events 1–31). A public-interface reproduction first timed out during environment setup, then showed `State(data=...)` raising `TypeError` (7, 12–13, 17).

The committed patch added `statemachine/data.py` with declarations and change records; accepted state data in `statemachine/state.py`; exported the types; and added per-machine storage, accessors, scope merging and history snapshots in `statemachine/statemachine.py` and the sync/async engines (33–44, 57, 123–124). It also added literal SCXML parsing and diagram annotations (85–86), and modified history serialization after a pickle failure (95–108). The final commit was `0d6a6b2`, 13 files changed (133–134). These actions span the requested interfaces, but their presence is not complete proof of their correctness.

## Validation, regressions and costs

A small entry/exit check showed initialization, removal and reset (67–68). Focused local suites reported 24 passes; then 89 passes and 142 skips; then 102 passes; and finally 93 passes after later edits (77–80, 91–93, 109–111, 127–129). Ruff and import checks passed (110–112, 125–126). These are local checks, **not** the official F2P/P2P results. The attempted 1,591-item local suite timed out after 120 seconds, and mypy timed out after formatting and lint passed (69–75). An initial focused invocation named a nonexistent diagram test and ran no tests (87–90).

The student investigated a history pickle error, identified `history_values` as the unpicklable object, changed serialization, and passed one history/pickle round trip (95–108). This was additional work prompted by a task-relevant persistence check, not established unnecessary work. The student also observed that callback mutation left stored data unchanged (113–114), disclosed it as a limitation (135), and corrected a separately observed after-transition scope example (121–126). No new committed test files appear in the final patch; several checks were ephemeral scripts. SCXML non-literal expressions are skipped by the added literal parser, and the visible record does not establish full downstream diagram or SCXML behavior.

The official `eval_output/python-statemachine-state-data-scoping/eval_result.json` and supplied verifier tail report three F2P failures: states without declared data returned `{}` rather than `None`, including sync and async transitions and backward compatibility. The single P2P failure was a weighted-transition statistical test timing out after 60 seconds. This is an observed regression in the official run, but its cause and whether it would recur are not established. The verifier completed in 233.65 seconds, below its 3,600-second timeout; the high partial score is not full success.

The run used 67 requested tool calls, 3,120,611 input and 8,140 output tokens, and 1,695.704 seconds wall time against a 1,800-second student budget. SDK `total_cost_usd` was **15.806555 as a proxy estimate**, not an actual CLI fee; no actual dollar fee is established. Timeouts, repeated checks and environment setup consumed time; the record does not quantify the marginal cost of any capability.

The tail does not establish the cause of every F2P failure or the single P2P failure. With an empty patch and no pre-attempt comparison supplied, the P2P failure cannot be attributed to a student-introduced regression; nor does the high P2P count establish full success.

None has directly observable executable behavior consistent with its candidate methods or verification steps in this attempt: there were no student tool calls. Event 1 only acknowledges that inspection and modification would be necessary; it does not claim application of any capability. Thus, preservation of continuation state, meaningful transition identity, and representation reconciliation were not demonstrated. This absence is contrary to performing their proposed inspections and checks, but it does not establish whether the model internally considered them. The trajectory is not presented as truncated; the **verifier output is a tail**, so details outside it are unassessable. Even a matching action would not by itself prove library causation, since an ordinary base student could perform it.

BEGIN_REPORT_TO_REVIEW
# Trajectory-to-Capability RSI：六小时封存实验与后续扩展报告

截至北京时间 2026-11-27 07:53 的事实快照，四个研究问题得到的证据并不一致：**轨迹能否提炼为可交付知识**——已形成带来源和适用条件的冻结文本，但不等于模型参数学会了知识；**状态对齐是否优于普通时间线总结**——匹配 V1 和事先选中的部署版本在新 Test 上均未表现出正式成功优势，且 V2 证据来源和交付长度不同，不能单独归因于对齐；**Hint 来源能否改善后续迁移**——四个历史轨迹来源库在一题上均正式未解，不能据此确定 Hint 风格的因果作用；**能否迁移到 Scene**——已有审计提供候选机制和失败链，但没有新的 Scene 迁移实证。样本极小，无正式统计显著性；同一任务下的多个参数化测试不是多个独立任务。

本稿仅呈现既有实验和审计，不继续实验、改动学生、能力库或评测，也不编写新任务解答。原六小时实验已封存；后续扩展的 29 条计划尝试已取得正式成绩，但快照时报告工作和资源关闭尚未完成。最终关闭记录、最后两次报告调用及最终成本以之后真实操作的附记为准，不在此预先声称新实验 VM 或服务已关闭。

## A. 两项工程审计：实际覆盖与证据边界

### A.1 Hint RSI：文件、分块与历史不可访问范围

扩展清单中可访问的 **117 份学生、104 份教师、36 份预检文件，共 257 份**，解码全文分为 **294 个块，均完成 CLI 模型文本审阅**，该历史全文队列未按字段或前缀截断。另捕获 116 份实际学生系统提示、117 份提示配置、28 份在线教师状态、12 份教师事件文件，并形成 20 个 guidance 审阅任务。文件、关联轮次、教师材料和副本不是独立尝试；模型文本审阅也不是全部程序重放或人工核验。原交接中不可访问的完整 rollout 仍未补齐，不纳入可访问分母。

原六小时报告的学生 **17/117**、教师 **4/104** 是当时截止状态，不能反写旧报告，也不能用来描述现时覆盖。另有 152 份历史 CSV 的 **1,130 条元数据记录**，包括 260 条尝试记录、860 条分组或 driver 汇总、10 条任务汇总以及计划、共享引用和缺失路径，既不是 1,130 次独立尝试，也不是全部深审的轨迹。详见[扩展 Hint 审计](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit_hintrsi.md)、[轨迹清单](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/trajectory_inventory.csv)和[历史记录表](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/historical_attempt_records.csv)。

**具体结果和干预链：**

- Claude 递归委派 D05 的教师要求检查旧上下文与新输入、成功和不同失败的反馈及调用 ID。学生事件 49–57 暴露并修改续接输入，84–85 检查结果和 ID；正式 **F2P 7/7、P2P 31/31**。后续图像上下文修改未被展示的同一探针单独验证，本地仍有 handler 失败；高度对应支持可能采纳，不证明教师因果贡献。[原轨迹](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/hint-codex-experiments/runs/codex_parallel_12/lanes/codex_parallel_12_lane0/codex_parallel_12_lane0_I_r2/infer_logs/claude-code-by-agents-recursive-delegation/turn_1/trajs.json)。
- D02 有实质实现和先失败后通过的窄 mock，正式 **6/7、31/31**，空文本案例未通过，内部原因未确定。D01 和 D04 则因调用的 Skill 所涉提供者歧义而停问、空补丁；D03 声称只读但无工具拒绝证据。H2 有非空复现脚本而无可见修复，不能与零字节补丁混同。它们不都是完成实现后的算法失败。
- 历史 B08 实际无任务教师 Hint，却正式 **7/7、31/31**；同批收到参考的 H、I 分别为 **6/7、2/7**。运行时和预算不构成严格受控比较，但反驳“教师是历史成功必要条件”。[B08 轨迹](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/hint-codex-experiments/runs/codex_parallel_08/lanes/codex_parallel_08_lane0/codex_parallel_08_lane0_B_r1/infer_logs/claude-code-by-agents-recursive-delegation/turn_1/trajs.json)。
- Igel D06 无任务教师参考，自主复现并实现 schema 保存和使用，正式 **23/24、2/2**；较早评价摘录指出 `feature_schema.joblib` 的生产者／消费者路径契约问题。D07 收到指导却因分支问题停问，**0/24、2/2**，替代分支存在不等于获得使用授权。[D06 审计](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/audit/audit_D06.md)。
- SQLfmt D08 是真实 source-13 接入预检而非正式九题成功：实现和聚焦检查后正式 **30/32、1271/1273**；一个可见间距差异不解释所有失败。历史另有 HTTP 400、进程退出 -11、取消及 API 402 后未评价等中断，缺失成绩不计为普通算法失败。[D08 审计](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/audit/audit_D08.md)。

实际捕获的系统提示优先于组名和可用 Hint 档案；收到、行为一致、因指导而行动、正式通过和长期能力是不同命题。

### A.2 Scene：索引、全文、像素和几何不是同一覆盖

- 规范主轨迹 **360/360 文件、571 分块**完成全文审阅；工作档案 454 条路径中，410 为原导出／脱敏副本、32 为解码文本完全副本，另 **12 份不同内容、12 块**完成审阅。不是 814 次独立实验。
- 27 个 tar 包的 **13,085 个成员已索引**；27 个归档控制块完成。执行映射涉及 1,483 个不同文件、1,644 条归档链接和 161 条轨迹链接，73 个执行块完成；另审阅 526 个选定控制文件和 722 个阶段／调用文件。索引、映射或归档保留不等于所有成员载荷深审；部分原始 API sidecar 未逐项重读。
- 新增视觉审阅为事先保存清单的六案：18 个图像引用中有六份不同参考内容、八份不同渲染内容；按版本字典序选择有渲染轨迹并跳过重复任务 ID，非随机或总体代表样本。全量像素、全部 `.blend` 二进制与精确网格关系未审。两个连续旧几何检查点**各**观察 874 对象、筛选 48 mesh、计算 1,128 对 AABB 下界；AABB 为零不证明接触或拓扑连接。

例如 Scene D01 教师指出植物可见性和显示器支撑疑问，补丁与阶段事件支持植物和面板实际修改，但遮挡、画外和过曝是竞争解释；抽样支架与底座未变，接触未知。S0087 第三步超时后保留第二步，S0078 第三步成功后仍选第二步；S0335 模型 CLI 超时且未开始 Blender，不是几何失败。S0354 局部锚点误差改善时另有误差变大。教师诊断、学生执行、候选接受、独立视觉质量必须分开。[Scene 全审报告及原始行号、归档成员、图像入口](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit_scene.md)；[主清单](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/scene_full_audit_manifest.json)；[视觉清单](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/scene_visual_expansion_manifest.json)。

## B. 原框架、实际入口与工程修复

原六小时实现是 `source_parallel_13` 的派生：复用原 SDK、Bridge、任务初始化、容器和评分器，通过 `capability_path` 将知识传给求解步骤，经已有 append-prompt 路径注入并保存实际加载文本；B0 走匹配的空追加路径，不注入知识。原有薄入口负责预存队列和 Hint 探索，不是重新训练学生或重写评分器。原工程曾修复能力路径未转发、空／非空追加的系统身份句不匹配，以及评价输出收集；被取代的尝试和成本保留。[原源码差异](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/results/source_changes.diff)。

后续扩展相对封存版本的源码差异清单仅列 `run_capability_experiments.py`：增加 Development、Hint-transfer、预存交错顺序、冻结检查、显式续跑及截止补丁首次评估处理；并未重写 Bridge、SDK、学生工具或评分逻辑。[扩展差异](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/results/source_changes_vs_closed_six_hour.diff)。

实际模型请求标识为 **`gpt-6.1-terra`、effort `low`**，不是全局默认型号；基础 CLI 形式为 `codex exec --ephemeral --json --ignore-user-config --sandbox read-only --model gpt-6.1-terra -c 'model_reasoning_effort="low"'`。完整实际命令见[命令记录](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/commands_used.md)。SDK 消息和工具经桥接序列化，不能宣称与原生 API 完全等价。实际首请求比较显示，同题组移除知识文本后公共系统文本、工具定义和非系统消息匹配；原生 Skill 禁用。未训练权重，只能保证已记录请求的模型标识和 effort 相同，服务端 checkpoint 不可见。

工程事件包括：原 VM socket 路径超过 UNIX 长度限制，复用原覆盖层并将 socket 移至短路径；镜像仓库 429 后保留失败并在镜像就绪后才启动学生；`-1` 超时哨兵修正为成绩未知；一次只读事后审查重复调用保留并计费。模型访问曾报 `model_not_found / model_not_in_accessible_catalog`，不是已证实额度耗尽；恢复前登录文件元数据更新，但操作者未改认证或型号，原因未定。受影响审计块以不同会话重发，已完成学生未重跑。用户中断后监控 PID 消失，核验后单独恢复控制器，保留监控空窗。[工程修复日志](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/logs/engineering_fixes.json)；[访问事件](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/model_access_incident.json)。

## C. 对照、能力库、预算与冻结

B0 不加载能力库。K0 仅用 Development 公开描述和教师一般先验，不看学生轨迹或解答；K1 沿时间线提炼检查、修改、探针和结果；K2 将**推断的**子目标与实际状态、操作和约束按跨任务功能对齐。V1 的 K1/K2 使用同一固定 D01–D08 队列；V2 则各自使用旧 V1、各自源自该队列的中间分析及两条各自在相同新 Development 题上生成的 on-policy 轨迹，故新证据不同，不能将 V2 差异单独归因于状态对齐。旧原始证据归档，增量更新时由先前分析代表。

共同提取上限为每条件两次调用、每次最多 700,000 字符、中间分析最多 2,200 词，交付最多 700 词和四个完整单元；同上限不等于实际消耗相同。新轨迹输入逐字段上限 1,800 字符、补丁上限 30,000 字符；新尝试事后审查同样有裁剪，不是历史全文分块审计。原 V1 的 K1/K2 使用相同机械过滤的 615,929 字符来源；提取字段修复前后的调用均留存并计入额外成本。

| 库 | 交付词数／单元 | 来源或部署状态 |
|---|---:|---|
| K0 V1 / V2 | 644／4；630／4 | 描述与先验；Selection 选 V2 |
| K1 V1 / V2 | 578／4；619／4 | 时间线；Selection 选 V2 |
| K2 V1 / V2 | 526／4；505／3 | 功能状态对齐；Selection 保留 V1 |
| HT_NoHint / Error / Strategy / Exploration | 688／4；654／4；677／4；647／4 | 分别来自四条历史 Development 轨迹 |

K2 V2 原稿 707 词、四单元，按事先规则整单元删除末项后交付 505 词、三单元；K1 V2 为 619 词、四单元。不能说等长，也不能把分析中被删的失败分类单元恢复成学生收到的内容。七个 V2 交付单元均以名称标题加六个标签满足七字段：适用问题、知识／行为约束、候选方法、验证步骤、不适用情形、证据来源。

K2 的具体区别是将 Cattrs 部分结果保留、旧续接和 schema 例子按“后续消费者需要何种状态”对齐；将 Snapshot 身份变化与 D05 请求／响应 ID 按功能联系；将类型修复及旧格式／schema 问题归为表示协调。它不是声称实现相同。K1 也要求保留已有结果、重跑探针和检查身份；K0 已包含生命周期、身份、schema、失败通道和合同检查等许多相同通用知识。状态对齐的强调与组织有区别，但不能仅凭措辞证明新增知识或行为收益。[机制比较](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/library_mechanism_comparison_pretest.md)；[冻结记录及全部主库路径](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/knowledge_freeze.json)；[HT 冻结记录](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/knowledge_freeze_hint.json)。

HT 名称表示知识来源；**HT_NoHint 仍加载从旧无 Hint 轨迹提取的知识，不是 B0**。四个新迁移测试均无在线 Hint。本次没有重新执行同预算的四种主动 Hint 生成试验，也没有共同中间状态反事实回放。

五个新任务的未见核查仅覆盖本机可访问历史元数据、精确任务 ID、公开需求及仓库明显重复；未读取 Test GT 做完整参考实现语义去重，也不能排除不可访问历史或模型预训练接触。[任务划分](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/task_split.json)；[公开需求筛查](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/public_duplicate_screen.md)。

## D. 封存结果、扩展成绩与成本

正式成功是评价器二元标准；F2P 为目标测试、P2P 为既有行为保持的辅助计数。部分正确不代替正式全解，正式通过也不保证完整真实世界契约。技术缺失或中断不按算法失败填零。

### D.1 原六小时封存结果：不可与后续 29 次合并

匹配 Selection 的 SuperJSON：B0、K1、K2 正式成功，K0 未解；K2 原评价缺新增测试报告、记 0，唯一一次同补丁完整诊断为 **80/80、116/116**，原结果和恢复成本保留。两道 Test 每组各一次：K0 **2/2**、K2 **1/2**、B0 和 K1 均 **0/2**。TOMLKit 的 K2 为 **59/60、964/964**，不是全解；HTTPX 的 B0、K1 各 **115/115、1280/1281**，仍正式未解。原可选开发 Hint：Error、Strategy、Exploration 各正式 **7/7、31/31**；NoHint 评价中断、成绩未知。原逐题 Token、调用、耗时、额外启动与异常见[封存完整报告](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/research_report_zh.md)和[原尝试指标](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_20261126_020200/attempt_metrics.csv)。

旧 NoHint 原约 600 秒中断与后续同补丁 900 秒诊断计时起点不同；后者仍超时，F2P/P2P 未知。已知好 Error 补丁对照 **7/7、31/31**，证明该路线可以完成，不证明 NoHint 根因查清；对照 stderr 的 Node 堆 OOM 与正式通过并存，其影响未知。[分诊](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/nohint_evaluator_triage_zh.md)。

### D.2 扩展：29 条计划尝试的正式结果

四次 Development、七次 Selection、十四次主 Test、四次 HT 均已取得正式成绩。下方独立占位行由渲染程序插入全部逐尝试 F2P/P2P、Input/Output/Cached、模型／工具调用、耗时、异常和额外评测成本；空字段表示未知。Input 已含 Cached，不能再相加。

### development：全部预定尝试

| 任务 | 组 | 状态 / 轨迹类别 | 正式成功 | F2P | P2P | 补丁字节 | 停止原因 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| cattrs-partial-structuring-recovery | K1_v1 | complete / 正式全通过 | 1 | 69/69 | 7/7 | 9329 | end_turn |

| Test：statemachine | 七个主组各 **0/1** | K0 V1/V2、K2 V1/V2 空补丁；B0、K1 V1/V2 有实现且各 69/72。全部 P2P 为 1285/1286，见共同基线诊断。 |
| HT：statemachine | NoHint、Error、Strategy、Exploration 各 **0/1** | 四组均 69/72、1285/1286；Strategy 为学生截止补丁首次评价，非正常结束。 |

**匹配 V1 对 V1：**新 Test 的 K1 V1 与 K2 V1 均 **1/2**；adaptix 均通过，statemachine 均未解，但 K1 有实现且 69/72，K2 空补丁 0/72。B0 和 K0 V1 也均 **1/2**。这些辅助差异不是正式成功差异，更不是因果效应。

**Selection 事先选择的部署版本：**K0 V2 因正式全解改善而保留；K1 V2 与 V1 正式成功相同，且 F2P、P2P 均未退化，故保留；K2 V2 未满足预先不退化规则，故保留 V1。选择不是依据 Test 成绩或解题成本。选中 K0 V2、K1 V2、K2 V1 在新 Test 均 **1/2**，但 K1 V2 与 K2 V1 是混合版本比较，不能当作状态对齐的纯因果效果。新 Test 未使用在线教师、GT 或旧会话，只加载各自冻结库；这不是脱离所有记忆后的参数能力证明。

首个 Selection 的 K1 V1 原 900 秒评价与一次 900 秒同补丁诊断均保留，诊断无完整计数；在其余 Selection 和任何新 Test 前统一将正式上限改为 **3600 秒**，补一次同补丁正式评价，学生未重跑，最终 **66/66、30014/30014**。初次 `wall_seconds` 仍为 1682.790 秒，含恢复等待的 `end_to_end_seconds` 为 12277.350 秒；诊断 912.661 秒、统一上限补评 1439.114 秒分别列账，不再重复加到端到端跨度。旧 Development 正式上限为 900 秒；学生上限始终 1800 秒。[预算修订](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/config/verification_budget_revision.json)。

**截止补丁与技术恢复。** Adaptix B0 和 HT_Strategy 均到 1800 秒 `student_timeout`，原编排器取消后未进入正式评价；随后仅对原样保存补丁进行**首次**正式评价，不是学生重跑或重复评测，保留原取消及等待成本。B0 正式通过不等于正常结束或已提交；Strategy 正式未解。薄队列政策对后续确认的同类截止统一适用，学生模型、工具、权限、预算、冻结库和测试不变；已运行进程可能仍加载旧调度代码，遇同类情况同样保留并继续首次评价。控制器因原子进程 PID 退出而单独恢复，不是学生重新开始。[B0 截止记录](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/runs/capability_followup_v2_test_adaptix-name-mapping-aliases_B0_r1/budget_stop_evaluation.json)；[Strategy 截止记录](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/runs/capability_followup_hint_transfer_hint_transfer_python-statemachine-state-data-scoping_HT_Strategy_r1/budget_stop_evaluation.json)。

**状态机共同基线问题。** 未修改 pristine base 也在 `test_statistical_distribution` 的既有测试触发 **60 秒单项 pytest 超时**；各完成运行唯一 P2P 失败 ID 与之相同，尚不能直接归为学生引入退化，也不能证明补丁没有性能影响。外层正式 verifier 上限为 3600 秒，相关 verifier 均正常结束；单项 60 秒不是外层中断。保留原正式分数，不排除该测试制造全通过。三个 F2P 失败另涉及无数据状态返回 `{}` 而非 `None`。冻结后的工程诊断不是 K2 学生或提取方法的发现，不能算 K2 迁移收益。[最新诊断](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/statemachine_baseline_timeout_diagnosis_zh.md)。

### D.3 用量与资源快照

截至快照，扩展记录 **1,857 个 CLI 请求，其中 1,748 个有用量**：Input **139,168,675**、Output **1,142,846**、Cached Input **9,114,752**，缓存已包含在 Input 内。未报告用量不能填零；分类包括历史文本审计、知识提取、学生各阶段、事后审计和报告等。历史 117 份学生文件中 108 份可关联 CLI Token，只有 56 份有逐 request 用量 ID；native CLI turn、Token 更新和 SDK 请求不是同一计数。原 SDK 美元为代理估计，不是真实 CLI 账单；结算美元未知。[历史成本表](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/historical_attempt_costs.csv)；[各角色会话表](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/historical_cli_usage_by_session.csv)。

资源采样 1,258 次，最大间隔约 6,674 秒，存在中断空窗；观测到自有容器同时最多四个、采样内存合计峰值约 4.17 GB，短峰可能漏测。宿主负载含其他用户，不归为本实验；VM RSS 已含访客容器，不能相加。报告与复核后最终账本和关闭附记优先于此快照。

## E. 采纳、行为变化、冗余与迁移失败

**输入可见不等于因果采纳。** 冻结库与首请求检查证明文本可用；行为与指导一致仍可能来自任务要求、源码、模型先验或普通工程实践。D05 教师问题后的针对性探针是较强的同轮行为一致证据，仍无隔离教师效应的对照。

- Cattrs K2 V1 的事件 63–64 检查修复失败字段时保留已有值；71–84 中泛型修复先破坏旧复现，再修正并重跑。该序列支持“状态保留、回归后重测”的语义提炼，不证明知识导致行动。K1 同题也做了复现和检查，事件 74–77 的自定义 hook／嵌套状态问题并未显示同一探针重跑。[K2 案例](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_development_cattrs-partial-structuring-recovery_K2_v1.md)。
- Aiomonitor 两组均实现并测试若干边界，却漏掉空列表反馈；K2 事件 33–45 的身份修改和探针不证明运行中到终止的完整身份匹配。两组局部 console 失败均缺可靠基线，不能臆断退化原因。[K1](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_development_aiomonitor-task-snapshots-diff_K1_v1.md)；[K2](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_development_aiomonitor-task-snapshots-diff_K2_v1.md)。
- Selection 的 B0 也自主复现、发现别名及默认值问题并修复；K2 V2 事件 41–44 的 helper 默认键改动最终造成 P2P 退化，尽管多次局部修复和 43 项选定测试通过。K1 V2 则发现同类默认键问题并在事件 33–42 修正。差异是可观察过程，不证明由库造成。[B0](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_selection_mashumaro-flattened-dataclass-fields_B0.md)；[K2 V2](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_selection_mashumaro-flattened-dataclass-fields_K2_v2.md)。
- Statemachine 中 K2 V2 一次请求、零工具、空补丁即提前结束；K0 V1/V2 和 K2 V1 则读仓库并复现缺失 API 后空补丁。它们不是实际算法实现失败的同一类型，也不能把低成本提前结束称为效率提升。B0 与 K1 两版本有实质实现但仍漏无数据合同；HT_NoHint、Error、Exploration 同样有实现但正式未解，Strategy 截止。HT_NoHint 事件 161–178 检查并修复回调修改不持久，临时复现最终删除；Exploration 事件 161–164 则观察到修改未持久。相同行为或分数不证明库产生收益。[HT_NoHint](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_hint_transfer_python-statemachine-state-data-scoping_HT_NoHint.md)；[HT_Exploration](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/posthoc_hint_transfer_python-statemachine-state-data-scoping_HT_Exploration.md)。

重复宽泛搜索、依赖阻塞、重跑测试和临时探针有真实代价；有些发现缺陷，有些未完成，不能一概称浪费或归因于库。普通 K1 总结和 K0 先验已有大量边界追踪、验证与状态知识；K2 的功能对齐是分析假设及实际文本差异，不自动构成独有能力。

## F. 值得迁往 Scene 的机制与待验证条件

仅提出候选机制：将消费者所需状态映射到对象身份、几何关系与场景阶段；把局部探针扩展为对象清点、精确接触／支撑检查及渲染检查；保留参考、修改前后状态、教师诊断、实际补丁与独立验收的链接；对相机、几何、材质、光照分别记录可能混杂。保留状态仅在合同要求连续性时适用，不能对所有对象机械套用。

验证前须固定或记录证据与预算，区分遮挡、出框和缺失，使用可复现的图像及几何检查，比较候选与普通总结及无库基线，并将提案复核、Selection 保留和独立质量验收分开。现有 Scene 的技术执行、有限像素观察及教师自评不足以证明上述机制有效；本次代码成绩不是 Scene 实证。

## G. 九项近邻工作与贡献边界

以下基于既有官方全文 CLI 审阅，不是复现或穷尽综述；论文结果为作者报告。[全文边界审阅](/srv/lab-disks/disk2/users/xymo/Documents/Codex/2026-11-26/trajectory-to-capability-rsi-hint-rsi/outputs/trajectory_capability_followup_20261126_182329/audit/related_work_fulltext_zh.md)。

1. [GEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19457)：用轨迹和反馈反思、演化并筛选提示；固定权重提示改进并非本研究首创。
2. [DoVer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06749)：在失败处恢复上下文与状态、干预重规划并验证假说；本研究没有完成相同中间状态的反事实回放。
3. [Trace2Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25158)：分析成功与失败轨迹并合并技能补丁；多轨迹总结和错误分析并非新颖点。
4. [SkillGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10999)：检索相近案例并用修复与退化评价候选；验证集参与选择，不等同独立 Test。
5. [SKILL-KD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28048)：对照教师与失败学生轨迹，用学生重跑验证技能；教师指导及重跑验证已有先例。
6. [Socratic Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17860)：学生测试建议并编辑资源，教育者审阅；教师—学生指导和跨会话技能并非首创。
7. [SimWorld Studio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09423)：从动作—观察失败形成规则，并结合场景图及视觉检查；其场景方法不证明本研究的 Scene 迁移。
8. [SceneEvolve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37046)：利用几何检查及开发评分选择工具／技能组合；整套候选选择不等于单项机制效果。
9. [Liu 和 Wu：Teaching the Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13144)：技能跟踪进度、语义与后置条件，候选须在匹配开发任务优于空库；其验证与本研究问题重叠。

较具体、仍待严格检验的问题是：**在相同证据和预算下，跨轨迹按功能等价状态与约束对齐，是否比时间线提炼带来增量价值**。现有 V1 较接近该比较，V2 因 on-policy 证据及交付覆盖不同不能隔离该因素；上述审阅未见明确隔离同一比较，并不证明其他工作不存在或本方法新颖。

**结语。** 已实现的是可追溯的历史全文审计、冻结文本知识与小样本新任务评价；观察到具体修复、正式通过、部分正确、空补丁、退化和技术中断并存。尚未验证状态对齐的独立因果收益、主动 Hint 风格效应、稳定效率收益或 Scene 迁移；后续最终成本和资源关闭状态应由真实操作附记补充。

END_REPORT_TO_REVIEW
